# Abacum | Dataset manager - Exploration

Raw catalog generated 2026-11-09 from the Figma Desktop Bridge. File: [SX7z0Cp4IMIw8RmNyrsI5G](https://www.figma.com/design/SX7z0Cp4IMIw8RmNyrsI5G). Every visible top-level node on every page is listed; frames inside sections are listed under the section. "Notes" is designer-placed text outside component instances (capped at 600 characters per frame); "UI" counts text strings inside instances; full text is in the JSON dump. Totals: 23 pages, 3827 frames/nodes, 280 image fills.
